	try {
		let pathSegments = [];
		try {
			pathSegments = window.location.pathname.split("/").filter((segment) => segment !== "" && segment !== "global").slice(0, 2);
		} catch {
			pathSegments = [];
		}
		const matchedLang = pathSegments.map((seg) => validLangs.find((lang) => lang.toLowerCase() === seg.toLowerCase())).find((lang) => lang !== void 0);
		const candidates = [];
		if (matchedLang) candidates.push(matchedLang);
		else candidates.push(defaultLang);
		candidates.push("global");
		let content = void 0;
		let usedLang = void 0;
		for (const key of candidates) {
			if (!key) continue;
			const banner = bannerByLocale[key];
			if (!banner?.content) continue;
			content = banner.content;
			usedLang = key;
			break;
		}
		if (!content) {
			document.documentElement.setAttribute(attr, "hidden");
			return;
		}
		let shouldShow = true;
		let i = 0;
		while (i < localStorage.length) {
			const key = localStorage.key(i);
			i++;
			if (!key?.endsWith(bannerDismissedKey)) continue;
			const langSpecificDismissal = localStorage.getItem(key);
			if (!!langSpecificDismissal && langSpecificDismissal === content) {
				shouldShow = false;
				break;
				//!key.startsWith("lang:")) makes this backwards compatible with removing legacy local storage
			} else if (usedLang && (key.startsWith(`lang:${usedLang}_`) || !key.startsWith("lang:"))) {
				localStorage.removeItem(key);
				i--;
			}
		}
		document.documentElement.setAttribute(attr, shouldShow ? "visible" : "hidden");
	} catch (e) {
		console.error(e);
		document.documentElement.setAttribute(attr, "hidden");
	}
})(

  [],
  "data-banner-state",
  "bannerDismissed",

	function setIsOpaque() {
		const offset = 50;
		let isOpaque = false;
		if (window.scrollY > offset) isOpaque = true;
		else isOpaque = false;
		const el = document.getElementById(navbarTransitionId);
		if (!el || !el.hasAttribute("data-is-opaque")) return;
		el.setAttribute("data-is-opaque", `${!!isOpaque}`);
	}
	/** https://github.com/pacocoursey/next-themes/blob/a7eeabc39cfb37d74ea3d82eac674d0e1851b1cb/next-themes/src/index.tsx#L234-L253 */
	function disableAnimation() {
		const css = document.createElement("style");
		const scope = `#${navbarTransitionId}`;
		css.appendChild(document.createTextNode(`${scope},${scope} *,${scope} *::before,${scope} *::after{-webkit-transition:none!important;-moz-transition:none!important;-o-transition:none!important;-ms-transition:none!important;transition:none!important}`));
		document.head.appendChild(css);
		return function() {
			window.getComputedStyle(document.body);
			setTimeout(() => {
				document.head.removeChild(css);
			}, 1);
		};
	}
	const restoreAnimation = disableAnimation();
	function done() {
		window.removeEventListener("scroll", setIsOpaque);
		document.removeEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", setIsOpaque);
		setIsOpaque();
		restoreAnimation();
	}
	window.addEventListener("scroll", setIsOpaque, { passive: true });
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", setIsOpaque);
	("requestAnimationFrame" in globalThis ? requestAnimationFrame : setTimeout)(setIsOpaque);
	if (document.readyState === "complete") done();
	else window.addEventListener("load", done, { once: true });
})("navbar-transition")</script><script id="_mintlify-scroll-margin-script">(function () {
  var measure = function measureScrollMarginTop() {
	const chromeElements = document.querySelectorAll("header#navbar, [data-top-chrome]");
	let chromeBottom = 0;
	chromeElements.forEach((element) => {
		const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
		if (rect.height > 0) chromeBottom = Math.max(chromeBottom, rect.bottom);
	});
	let containerTop = 0;
	const contentContainer = document.getElementById("content-container");
	if (contentContainer) {
		const { overflowY } = getComputedStyle(contentContainer);
		if (overflowY === "auto" || overflowY === "scroll") containerTop = Math.max(0, contentContainer.getBoundingClientRect().top);
	}
	const scrollMt = `${Math.round(Math.max(0, chromeBottom - containerTop)) + 40}px`;
	const rootStyle = document.documentElement.style;
	if (rootStyle.getPropertyValue("--scroll-mt") !== scrollMt) rootStyle.setProperty("--scroll-mt", scrollMt);
};
  (function watchScrollMarginTop(measure) {
	let observed = [];
	let frame = 0;
	const resizeObserver = typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined" ? null : new ResizeObserver(schedule);
	function run() {
		const elements = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("header#navbar, [data-top-chrome]"));
		if ((elements.length !== observed.length || elements.some((element, index) => element !== observed[index])) && resizeObserver) {
			resizeObserver.disconnect();
			elements.forEach((element) => resizeObserver.observe(element));
		}
		observed = elements;
		measure();
	}
	function schedule() {
		if (frame) return;
		frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
			frame = 0;
			run();
		});
	}
	const chromeSelector = "header#navbar, [data-top-chrome]";
	function touchesChrome(mutation) {
		const target = mutation.target;
		if (target instanceof Element && target.closest(chromeSelector)) return true;
		for (const node of mutation.addedNodes) if (node instanceof Element && (node.matches(chromeSelector) || node.querySelector(chromeSelector))) return true;
		for (const node of mutation.removedNodes) if (node instanceof Element && (node.matches(chromeSelector) || node.querySelector(chromeSelector))) return true;
		return false;
	}
	new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
		if (mutations.some(touchesChrome)) schedule();
	}).observe(document.documentElement, {
		childList: true,
		subtree: true
	});
	window.addEventListener("resize", schedule);
	if (document.readyState === "loading") document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", run);
	run();
})(measure);
  (function pinInitialHash(measure) {
	const hash = window.location.hash.slice(1);
	if (!hash) return;
	function isSmooth() {
		return getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).scrollBehavior === "smooth";
	}
	function pin(behavior) {
		const target = document.getElementById(hash);
		if (!target) return false;
		measure();
		target.scrollIntoView(behavior ? { behavior } : void 0);
		return true;
	}
	if (document.readyState === "complete") {
		pin();
		return;
	}
	const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
		if (!isSmooth() && pin("instant")) stop();
	});
	function stop() {
		observer.disconnect();
		window.removeEventListener("load", onLoad);
	}
	function settle() {
		const inputEvents = [
			"wheel",
			"touchstart",
			"keydown",
			"pointerdown"
		];
		let ticks = 0;
		let lastY = window.scrollY;
		const interval = setInterval(() => {
			ticks += 1;
			const target = document.getElementById(hash);
			if (!target || ticks >= 6) return done();
			const scrolling = Math.abs(window.scrollY - lastY) > 1;
			lastY = window.scrollY;
			if (scrolling) return;
			const margin = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(target).scrollMarginTop) || 0;
			if (Math.abs(target.getBoundingClientRect().top - margin) > 4) pin();
		}, 350);
		function done() {
			clearInterval(interval);
			inputEvents.forEach((event) => window.removeEventListener(event, done));
		}
		inputEvents.forEach((event) => window.addEventListener(event, done, { passive: true }));
	}
	function onLoad() {
		const smooth = isSmooth();
		stop();
		if (smooth) requestAnimationFrame(() => {
			pin();
			settle();
		});
	}
	window.addEventListener("load", onLoad);
	observer.observe(document.documentElement, {
		childList: true,
		subtree: true
	});
	if (!isSmooth() && pin("instant")) stop();
})(measure);
})();</script><noscript><iframe src="https://www.googletagmanager.com/ns.html?id=GTM-52W7VM2" height="0" width="0" style="display:none;visibility:hidden"></iframe></noscript><a href="#content-area" class="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:fixed focus:top-2 focus:left-2 focus:z-50 focus:p-2 focus:text-sm focus:bg-background-light dark:focus:bg-background-dark focus:rounded-md focus:outline-primary dark:focus:outline-primary-light">Skip to main content</a><script>(function assistantScriptFn(storageKey, widthKey, cssVar, hasChatPermissions, isAssistantUiV2) {

  try {
    if (window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone) return;

    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content') && matches[j].getClientRects().length) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;
    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var row = item.querySelector(':scope > button') || item;
    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = row.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

<span data-as="p">All joins are generated as <code>LEFT JOIN</code>. The cube which defines the join serves
as a main table, and any cubes referenced inside the <code>joins</code> property are used
in the <code>LEFT JOIN</code> clause. Learn more about direction of joins
<a href="/docs/data-modeling/joins#direction-of-joins" class="link">here</a>.</span>
<span data-as="p">The semantics of <code>INNER JOIN</code> can be achieved with additional filtering. For
example, a simple check of whether the column value <code>IS NOT NULL</code> by using <a href="/reference/core-data-apis/rest-api/query-format#set" class="link">set
filter</a> satisfies this requirement.</span>
<span data-as="p">There’s also no way to define <code>FULL OUTER JOIN</code> and <code>RIGHT OUTER JOIN</code> for the
sake of join modeling simplicity. To get <code>RIGHT OUTER JOIN</code> semantics just
define join <a href="/docs/data-modeling/joins#direction-of-joins" class="link">from other side of relationship</a>.
The <code>FULL OUTER JOIN</code> can be built inside cube <a href="/reference/data-modeling/cube#sql" class="link">sql</a>
parameter. Quite frequently, <code>FULL OUTER JOIN</code> is used to solve <a href="/docs/data-modeling/concepts/data-blending#data-blending" class="link">Data
Blending</a> or similar problems. In that case, it’s best
practice to have a separate cube for such an operation.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="parameters"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#parameters" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Parameters</span></h2>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="name"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#name" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">name</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The name must match the name of the joined cube and, thus, follow the <a href="/docs/data-modeling/concepts/syntax#naming" class="link">naming
conventions</a>.</span>
<span data-as="p">For example, when the <code>products</code> cube is joined on to the <code>orders</code> cube, we
would define the join as follows:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border dark:border-white/10 min-w-0 <span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">})</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="alias"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#alias" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">alias</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>alias</code> parameter gives a join its own name, so that a cube can join the
same cube more than once, each time on different columns. It’s the way to model
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dimension_(data_warehouse)#Role-playing_dimension" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">role-playing dimensions</a>: for example, an <code>orders</code>
cube that references <code>users</code> both as the customer and as the manager, or a fact
table that joins one <a href="/docs/data-modeling/concepts/calendar-cubes#joining-on-several-date-columns" class="link">calendar cube</a> on
several date columns.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Warning" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="warning"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="flex-none size-5 text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutWarning"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Join aliases are powered by Tesseract, the <a href="https://cube.dev/blog/introducing-next-generation-data-modeling-engine" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">next-generation data modeling
engine</a>, and are available since v1.8.7.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p"><code>alias</code> is only available when <code>joins</code> is defined as a list, which is the
default in YAML. In JavaScript, define <code>joins</code> as an array of objects with a
<code>name</code> instead of an object keyed by cube name.</span>

**:outline-0 **:focus:ring-0 **:focus:outline-0 rounded-xt bg-codeblock"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_dgt5deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] <span data-as="p">Each aliased join is a separate copy of the joined cube in the generated SQL:</span>

numberOfLines="2"><span class="line"><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">LEFT JOIN</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> users </span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">AS</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;orders__customer&quot;</span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> ON</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;orders&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.customer_id = </span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;orders__customer&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.id</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">LEFT JOIN</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> users </span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">AS</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;orders__manager&quot;</span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> ON</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;orders&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.manager_id = </span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;orders__manager&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.id</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Inside the <code>sql</code> of an aliased join, <code>{CUBE}</code> refers to the declaring cube and
the alias refers to the joined copy. The name of the joined cube (<code>{users}</code>
above) refers to the joined copy, too, except in a self-join, where it refers to
the declaring cube. Referencing the alias makes the intent explicit.</span>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="querying-members-through-an-alias"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#querying-members-through-an-alias" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Querying members through an alias</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">Members of an aliased join are referenced by a path that starts at the
declaring cube, followed by the alias and the member name. In the example above,
<code>orders.customer.city</code> and <code>orders.manager.city</code> are the cities of the customer
and of the manager:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-50 ring-1 ring-transparent dark:ring-white/[0.14] codeblock-dark twoslash-dark dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent dark:bg-transparent" data-has-floating-buttons="" numberOfLines="4" language="json"><div data-floating-buttons="true" class="absolute top-3 right-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden"><div class="code-block-copy-button z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_1u995deehkd74t9_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center aria-label="Copy the contents from the code block"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4 shrink-0 text-white/40 group-hover/copy-button:text-white/60"><use leading-6"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes dark-plus dark-plus" style="background-color:#0B0C0E;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="json"><code language="json" numberOfLines="4"><span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  &quot;measures&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: [</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;orders.count&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  &quot;dimensions&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: [</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;orders.customer.city&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;orders.manager.city&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Paths can go through joins of the aliased cube, e.g.,
<code>orders.customer.departments.name</code>. A time dimension reached through an alias
can be used in <code>timeDimensions</code> with a <code>granularity</code>, or referenced with a
granularity directly, e.g., <code>orders.customer.created_at.month</code>.</span>
<span data-as="p">Members of the declaring cube can reference an alias in their <code>sql</code>, the same
way as any joined cube:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-50 ring-1 ring-transparent dark:ring-white/[0.14] codeblock-dark twoslash-dark dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent dark:bg-transparent" data-has-floating-buttons="" numberOfLines="4" language="yaml"><div data-floating-buttons="true" class="absolute top-3 right-4 flex items-center class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md group/ask-assistant-button [.code-block_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-gray-800 [.code-block:has([data-fade-overlay])_[data-floating-buttons]_&amp;]:bg-transparent" id="ask-assistant-code-block-button" aria-label="Ask Assistant" data-chat-payload-element-id="lang-yaml-code-dimensions____-_name"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4 shrink-0 text-white/40 **:outline-0 **:focus:ring-0 **:focus:outline-0 rounded-2xl bg-codeblock"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_1hf5deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] py-3.5 px-4 overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="min-w-full! h-full children:my-0! children:shadow-none! children:bg-transparent!"><div class="font-mono whitespace-pre leading-6"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes dark-plus dark-plus" style="background-color:#0B0C0E;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="yaml"><code language="yaml" numberOfLines="4"><span class="line"><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">dimensions</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  - </span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">name</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">customer_city</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">    sql</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;{customer.city}&quot;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">    type</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">string</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">An aliased join is only reachable through its alias. In the example above,
querying <code>orders.count</code> together with <code>users.city</code> doesn’t use either of the
aliased joins, and fails unless there’s another join path from <code>orders</code> to
<code>users</code>.</span>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="using-join-aliases-in-views"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#using-join-aliases-in-views" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Using join aliases in views</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">To include members of an aliased join in a <a href="/reference/data-modeling/view" class="link">view</a>, use the alias in
the <code>join_path</code>. Use <code>prefix</code> or <code>alias</code> on the included members to tell copies
of the same cube apart:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-50 ring-1 ring-transparent dark:ring-white/[0.14] codeblock-dark twoslash-dark dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent dark:bg-transparent" data-has-floating-buttons="" numberOfLines="16" language="yaml"><div data-floating-buttons="true" class="absolute top-3 right-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden"><div class="code-block-copy-button z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_1u9n5deehkd74t9_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center leading-6"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes dark-plus dark-plus" style="background-color:#0B0C0E;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="yaml"><code language="yaml" <span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  - </span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">name</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">orders_view</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">        prefix</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">true</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">        includes</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">          - </span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">city</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">      - </span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">join_path</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">orders.manager</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">        prefix</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">true</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">        includes</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">          - </span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">city</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">This view exposes <code>customer_city</code> and <code>manager_city</code>. Members reached through
an alias are not listed in the metadata of the cube itself, so the <a href="/reference/core-data-apis/sql-api" class="link">SQL
API</a> and Playground reach them through views.</span>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="rules-and-limitations"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#rules-and-limitations" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Rules and limitations</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">Several joins to the same cube are allowed only when each of them has an
<code>alias</code>. An alias must be unique within its cube, and it can’t be:</span>
<ul>
<li>the name of the joined cube, or of any other cube in the data model;</li>
<li>the name of a member of the declaring cube, <code>CUBE</code>, or <code>TABLE</code>;</li>
<li>a name that contains <code>__</code>, or that renders under the same SQL alias as
another alias or cube, e.g., <code>customerCity</code> and <code>customer_city</code>.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">A cube that <a href="/reference/data-modeling/cube#extends" class="link"><code>extends</code></a> another one can’t redeclare one of its
aliased joins.</span>
<span data-as="p">Access policies of a cube apply to every copy of it that a query reads: member
access is checked against the cube, while row filters and masks constrain each
copy independently.</span>
<span data-as="p"><a href="/docs/pre-aggregations/matching-pre-aggregations" class="link">Pre-aggregations</a> defined in the joined cube don’t serve
queries that reach it through an alias. A pre-aggregation of the declaring cube
can reference a member through an alias, e.g., <code>time_dimension: customer.created_at</code>, and serves queries by that alias. A <code>rollup_join</code> can’t
reference a member through an alias.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="relationship"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#relationship" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">relationship</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>relationship</code> property is used to describe the type of the relationship
between joined cubes. It’s important to properly define the type of relationship
so Cube can accurately calculate measures.</span>
<span data-as="p">The cube that declares the join is considered <em>left</em> in terms of the <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Join_(SQL)#Left_outer_join" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">left
join</a> semantics, and the joined cube is considered <em>right</em>. It
means that all rows of the <em>left</em> cube are selected, while only those rows of
the <em>right</em> cube that match the condition are selected as well. For more
information and specific examples, please see <a href="/docs/data-modeling/joins#direction-of-joins" class="link">join
directions</a>.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Info" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 dark:border-neutral-700 dark:bg-white/10" data-callout-type="info"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="flex-none size-5 text-neutral-800 dark:text-neutral-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutInfo"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-neutral-800 dark:text-neutral-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">The join does not need to be defined on both cubes, but the definition can
affect the <a href="/docs/data-modeling/joins#direction-of-joins" class="link">join direction</a>.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">You can use the following types of relationships:</span>
<ul>
<li><code>one_to_one</code> for <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One-to-one_(data_model)" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">one-to-one</a> relationships</li>
<li><code>one_to_many</code> for <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One-to-many_(data_model)" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">one-to-many</a> relationships</li>
<li><code>many_to_one</code> for the opposite of <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One-to-many_(data_model)" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">one-to-many</a> relationships</li>
</ul>
<div role="note" aria-label="Warning" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="warning"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="flex-none size-5 text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutWarning"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">The types of relationships listed above were introduced in v0.32.19 for clarity
as they are commonly used in the data space. The following aliases were used
before and are still valid, so there’s no need to update existing data models:</span><ul>
<li><code>one_to_one</code> was known as <code>has_one</code> or <code>hasOne</code></li>
<li><code>one_to_many</code> was known as <code>has_many</code> or <code>hasMany</code></li>
<li><code>many_to_one</code> was known as <code>belongs_to</code> or <code>belongsTo</code></li>
</ul></div></div>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="one-to-one"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#one-to-one" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">One-to-one</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">The <code>one_to_one</code> type indicates a <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One-to-one_(data_model)" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">one-to-one</a> relationship between
the declaring cube and the joined cube. It means that one row in the declaring
cube can match only one row in the joined cube.</span>
<span data-as="p">For example, in a data model containing <code>users</code> and their <code>profiles</code>, the
<code>users</code> cube would declare the following join:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border dark:border-white/10 min-w-0 data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content"><div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" role="tablist" aria-label="Code examples" class="text-xs leading-6 gap-1 flex"><button type="button" data-active="" data-orientation="horizontal" <h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="one-to-many"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#one-to-many" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">One-to-many</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">The <code>one_to_many</code> type indicates a <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One-to-many_(data_model)" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">one-to-many</a> relationship between
the declaring cube and the joined cube. It means that one row in the declaring
cube can match many rows in the joined cube.</span>
<span data-as="p">For example, in a data model containing <code>authors</code> and the <code>books</code> they have
written, the <code>authors</code> cube would declare the following join:</span>

<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="many-to-one"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#many-to-one" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Many-to-one</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">The <code>many_to_one</code> type indicates the many-to-one relationship between the
declaring cube and the joined cube. You’ll often find this type of relationship
on the opposite side of the <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One-to-many_(data_model)" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">one-to-many</a> relationship. It means that
one row in the declaring cube matches a single row in the joined cube, while a
row in the joined cube can match many rows in the declaring cube.</span>
<span data-as="p">For example, in a data model containing <code>orders</code> and <code>customers</code> who made them,
the <code>orders</code> cube would have the following join:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border dark:border-white/10 min-w-0 <h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="sql"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#sql" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">sql</span></h3>
<span data-as="p"><code>sql</code> is necessary to indicate a related column between cubes. It is important
to properly specify a matching column when creating joins. Take a look at the
example below:</span>

h-full children:my-0! children:shadow-none! children:bg-transparent!"><div class="font-mono whitespace-pre flex-none text-sm h-full leading-6" data-component-part="code-group-tab-content"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes dark-plus dark-plus" style="background-color:#0B0C0E;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="javascript"><code language="javascript" numberOfLines="12"><span class="line"><span style="color:#DCDCAA;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">cube</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">`orders`</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A9955;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">  // ...</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  joins:</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    customers:</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      relationship:</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> `many_to_one`</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A9955;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">      // The `customer_id` column of the `orders` cube corresponds to the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6A9955;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">      // `id` dimension of the `customers` cube</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      sql:</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> `</span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">${</span><span style="color:#4FC1FF;--shiki-dark:#4FC1FF">CUBE</span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">}</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">.customer_id = </span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">${</span><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">customers</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.</span><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">id</span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">}</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">})</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="setting-a-primary-key"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#setting-a-primary-key" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Setting a primary key</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">In order for a join to work, it is necessary to define a <code>primary_key</code> as
specified below. It is a requirement when a join is defined so that Cube can
handle row multiplication issues such as chasm and fan traps.</span>
<span data-as="p">Let’s imagine you want to calculate <code>Order Amount</code> by <code>Order Item Product Name</code>.
In this case, <code>Order</code> rows will be multiplied by the <code>Order Item</code> join due to
the <code>one_to_many</code> relationship. In order to produce correct results, Cube will
select distinct primary keys from <code>Order</code> first and then will join these primary
keys with <code>Order</code> to get the correct <code>Order Amount</code> sum result. Please note that
<code>primary_key</code> should be defined in the <code>dimensions</code> section.</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border dark:border-white/10 min-w-0 border-transparent bg-codeblock dark:bg-white/5 text-gray-50 codeblock-dark"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 relative pr-2.5" data-component-part="code-group-tab-bar"><div <span data-as="p">If you don’t have a single column in a cube’s table that can act as a primary
key, you can create a composite primary key as shown below.</span>

depending on your database.</span></div></div>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="chasm-and-fan-traps"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#chasm-and-fan-traps" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Chasm and fan traps</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Cube automatically detects chasm and fan traps based on the <code>many_to_one</code> and <code>one_to_many</code> relationships defined in join.
When detected, Cube generates a deduplication query that evaluates all distinct primary keys within the multiplied measure’s cube and then joins distinct primary keys to this cube on itself to calculate the aggregation result.
If there’s more than one multiplied measure in a query, then such query is generated for every such multiplied measure, and results are joined.
Cube solves for chasm and fan traps during query time.
If there’s pre-aggregregation that fits measure multiplication requirements it’d be leveraged to serve such a query.
Such pre-aggregations and queries are always considered non-additive for the purpose of pre-aggregation matching.</span>
<span data-as="p">Let’s consider an example data model:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border dark:border-white/10 min-w-0 border-transparent bg-codeblock dark:bg-white/5 text-gray-50 codeblock-dark"><div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 relative pr-2.5" data-component-part="code-group-tab-bar"><div <span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      sql:</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> `id`</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      type:</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> `number`</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">      primary_key:</span><span style="color:#569CD6;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> true</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">})</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">If we try to query <code>customers.average_age</code> by <code>orders.city</code>, the Cube detects that the <code>average_age</code> measure in the <code>customers</code> cube would be multiplied by <code>orders</code> to <code>customers</code> and would generate SQL similar to:</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="cube-reference"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#cube-reference" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">CUBE reference</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">When you have several joined cubes, you should accurately use columns’ names to
avoid any mistakes. One way to make no mistakes is to use the <code>CUBE</code> reference.
It allows you to specify columns’ names in cubes without any ambiguity. During
the implementation of the query, this reference will be used as an alias for a
basic cube. Take a look at the following example:</span>

</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="transitive-joins"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#transitive-joins" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Transitive joins</span></h2>
<div role="note" aria-label="Warning" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="warning"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="flex-none size-5 text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutWarning"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Join graph is directed and <code>a → b</code> join is different from <code>b → a</code>. <a href="/docs/data-modeling/joins#direction-of-joins" class="link">Learn more
about it here</a>.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Cube automatically takes care of transitive joins. For example, consider the
following data model:</span>

<span data-as="p">Assume that the following query is run:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-50 ring-1 ring-transparent dark:ring-white/[0.14] codeblock-dark twoslash-dark dark:twoslash-dark bg-transparent dark:bg-transparent" data-has-floating-buttons="" numberOfLines="4" language="json"><div data-floating-buttons="true" class="absolute top-3 right-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden"><div class="code-block-copy-button z-10 select-none" id="base-ui-_R_1ud15deehkd74t9_" data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger=""><button class="size-6.5 flex items-center justify-center data-component-part="code-block-root" class="w-0 min-w-full max-w-full h-full dark:bg-codeblock text-sm leading-6 transition-[height] duration-300 ease-in-out code-block-background **:ring-0 [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] py-3.5 px-4 overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="min-w-full! h-full children:my-0! children:shadow-none! children:bg-transparent!"><div class="font-mono whitespace-pre leading-6"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes dark-plus dark-plus" style="background-color:#0B0C0E;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="json"><code language="json" numberOfLines="4"><span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  &quot;measures&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: [</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;a.count&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#9CDCFE;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  &quot;dimensions&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: [</span><span style="color:#CE9178;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;c.category&quot;</span><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D4D4D4;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#0B0C0E;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Joins <code>a → b</code> and <code>b → c</code> will be resolved automatically. Cube uses the
<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dijkstra%27s_algorithm" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Dijkstra algorithm</a> to find a join path between cubes given
requested members.</span>
<span data-as="p">In case there are multiple join paths that can be used to join the same set of cubes, Cube will collect cube names from members in the following order:</span>
<ol>
<li>Measures</li>
<li>Dimensions</li>
<li>Segments</li>
<li>Time dimensions</li>
</ol>
<span data-as="p">Cube makes join trees as predictable and stable as possible, but this isn’t guaranteed in case multiple join paths exist.
Please use views to address join predictability and stability.</span></div><nav id="pagination" data-layout-item="pagination" aria-label="Pagination" class="px-0.5 flex items-center gap-6 text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200"><a href="/reference/data-modeling/segments" rel="prev" aria-label="Previous: Segments" data-component-part="pagination-prev" class="group flex `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`one_to_many`}),` relationships defined in join.\nWhen detected, Cube generates a deduplication query that evaluates all distinct primary keys within the multiplied measure’s cube and then joins distinct primary keys to this cube on itself to calculate the aggregation result.\nIf there’s more than one multiplied measure in a query, then such query is generated for every such multiplied measure, and results are joined.\nCube solves for chasm and fan traps during query time.\nIf there’s pre-aggregregation that fits measure multiplication requirements it’d be leveraged to serve such a query.\nSuch pre-aggregations and queries are always considered non-additive for the purpose of pre-aggregation matching.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Let’s
